TL;DR: H3D-MarNet是一个两阶段框架，通过小波预处理抑制金属伪影，再结合CNN-Transformer混合架构实现kVCT到MVCT的域转换，在临床CT图像中取得了28.14 dB PSNR和0.717 SSIM的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT图像中的金属伪影严重降低图像质量，影响诊断准确性和放疗计划制定，尤其对于有高密度植入物的癌症患者，亟需有效的伪影抑制与CT模态转换方法。

Method: 提出H3D-MarNet两阶段框架：第一阶段用小波预处理模块进行频域感知去噪；第二阶段用Domain-TransNet，包含CNN编码器提取局部细节、Transformer编码器建模长程依赖，通过注意力特征融合机制整合互补表征，并采用多阶段注意力引导解码器与深度监督进行渐进式重建。

Result: 在受伪影影响的切片上达到28.14 dB PSNR和0.717 SSIM，有效抑制金属伪影并保留解剖结构。

Conclusion: H3D-MarNet在临床放疗工作流中具有可靠的CT模态转换潜力，为金属伪影抑制和解剖结构保留提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Metal artifacts in computed tomography (CT) severely degrade image quality, compromising diagnostic accuracy and radiotherapy planning, especially in cancer patients with high-density implants. We propose H3D-MarNet, a two-stage framework for artifact-aware CT domain transformation from kilo-voltage CT (kVCT) to mega-voltage CT (MVCT). In the first stage, a wavelet-based preprocessing module suppresses metal-induced artifacts through frequency-aware denoising while preserving anatomical structures. In second stage, Domain-TransNet performs kVCT-to-MVCT domain transformation using a hybrid volumetric learning architecture. Domain-TransNet integrates a CNN-based encoder to capture fine-grained local anatomical details and a transformer-based encoder to model long-range volumetric dependencies. The complementary representations are fused through an attention-based feature fusion mechanism to ensure spatial and contextual coherence across slices. A multi-stage, attention-guided decoder, supported by deep supervision, progressively reconstructs artifact-suppressed MVCT volumes.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that H3D-MarNet achieves 28.14 dB PSNR and 0.717 SSIM on artifact-affected slices from full dataset, indicating effective metal artifact suppression and anatomical preservation, highlighting its potential for reliable CT modality transformation in clinical radiotherapy workflows.

</details>


### [110] [From Image Hashing to Scene Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12259)
*Anh-Kiet Duong,Marie-Claire Iatrides,Petra Gomez-Krämer,Jean-Michel Carozza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HashSCD框架，将补丁级哈希编码与XOR聚合操作结合，实现高效的无监督场景变化检测与空间定位，在保持竞争力的同时大幅降低计算与存储成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像哈希方法仅限于全局比较，无法定位变化发生的空间位置，因此不适用于需要空间定位的场景变化检测任务。

Method: 采用补丁级哈希编码（patch-wise hashing），将空间对齐的图像块编码为紧凑哈希码，通过类XOR操作在汉明空间中直接进行变化检测与定位；模型以无监督方式训练，在补丁级和全局级同时应用对比学习。

Result: 与现有最先进的无监督哈希方法和场景变化检测方法相比，HashSCD取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时显著降低了计算成本和存储需求。

Conclusion: 补丁级哈希框架能够有效弥合哈希与场景变化检测之间的鸿沟，在保持高效性的同时实现空间定位能力，为哈希在变化检测领域的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Image hashing provides compact representations for efficient storage and retrieval but is inherently limited to global comparison and cannot reason about where changes occur. This limitation prevents hashing from being directly applicable to scene change detection, where spatial localization is essential. In this work, we revisit hashing from a scene change detection perspective and propose HashSCD, a patch-wise hashing framework that enables both efficient global change detection and localized change identification. HashSCD encodes spatially aligned patches into compact hash codes and aggregates them through an XOR-like operation, allowing change detection and localization to be performed directly in the Hamming space without repeated inference on previous images. The model is trained in an unsupervised manner using contrastive learning at both patch and global levels. Experiments demonstrate that HashSCD achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art unsupervised hashing and scene change detection methods, while significantly reducing computational cost and storage requirements.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Text Prompts: Visual-to-Visual Generation as A Unified Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12271)
*Yaofang Liu,Kangning Cui,Meng Chu,Zhaoqing Li,Suiyun Zhang,Jean-Michel Morel,Xiaodong Cun,Haoxuan Che,Rui Liu,Raymond H. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉到视觉（V2V）生成范式，通过V2V-Zero框架利用冻结VLM的隐藏状态实现无需训练的视觉条件生成，在GenEval上达0.85，并通过Simple-V2V Bench在7个视觉条件任务上评估7个模型，机制分析显示95%的注意力集中在视觉页面隐藏状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成器要求用户将视觉意图序列化为文本提示，这一瓶颈压缩了空间结构、精确外观和字形等信号，作者希望绕过文本序列化，直接以视觉规范页面作为条件。

Method: 提出V2V-Zero，一个无需训练框架，通过用视觉页面的最终层隐藏状态替换文本条件，利用冻结VLM已将文本和图像映射到生成器条件空间的事实，实现视觉到视觉生成。

Result: GenEval上达0.85，接近优化后的文生图性能；Simple-V2V Bench上V2V-Zero得分32.7/100，优于开源基线；视频扩展模型HunyuanVideo-1.5得分20.2/100。机制分析显示95%的 conditioning token 注意力集中在视觉页面隐藏状态。

Conclusion: V2V范式有效绕过文本瓶颈，属性绑定能力强但内容生成不可靠，结构控制仍有挑战。该接口可扩展至视频生成，揭示了现有模型在视觉条件生成上的能力层级。

Abstract: Humans often specify and create through visual artifacts: typography sheets, sketches, reference images, and annotated scenes. Yet modern visual generators still ask users to serialize this intent into text, a bottleneck that compresses signals like spatial structure, exact appearance, and glyph shape. We propose \textbf{\emph{visual-to-visual} (V2V)} generation, in which the user conditions a generative model with a visual specification page rather than a text prompt. The page is not an edit target, but a visual document that specifies the desired output. We introduce \textbf{V2V-Zero}, a training-free framework that exposes this interface in existing vision-language model (VLM) conditioned generators by replacing text-only conditioning with final-layer hidden states extracted from visual pages, exploiting the fact that the frozen VLM already maps both text and images into the generator's conditioning space.
  On GenEval, V2V-Zero reaches 0.85 with a frozen Qwen-Image backbone, closely matching its optimized text-to-image performance without fine-tuning. To evaluate the broader V2V space, we introduce \textbf{Simple-V2V Bench}, spanning seven visual-conditioning tasks and seven models, including GPT Image 2, Nano Banana 2, Seedream 5.0 Lite, open-weight baselines, and a video extension. V2V-Zero scores 32.7/100, outperforming evaluated open-weight image baselines and revealing a clear capability hierarchy: attribute binding is strong, content generation is unreliable, and structural control remains hard even for commercial systems. A HunyuanVideo-1.5 extension scores 20.2/100, showing the interface transfers beyond images. Mechanistic analysis shows the default reasoning path is primarily visually routed, with 95.0\% of conditioning-token attention mass on visual-page hidden states.

</details>


### [112] [Large-Small Model Collaboration for Farmland Semantic Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12282)
*Xinjia Li,Rui Wang,Qiurong Peng,Lingfei Ye,Dengrong Zhang,Haoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模细粒度农田语义变化检测基准HZNU-FCD，以及一个大小模型协同的变化检测框架（FD-Mamba + CMLA），在多个数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农田语义变化检测数据集缺乏细粒度"从-到"标注，且视觉变化检测模型易受物候伪变化（作物轮作、季节变化、光照差异）干扰，难以满足精细化农田转用监测需求。

Method: 构建HZNU-FCD基准（4588对双时相图像，五类农田转非农田标注协议）；提出大小模型协同框架：小模型FD-Mamba学习密集变化表征进行边界保持与小区域定位，大模型CMLA引入CLIP文本先验进行语义仲裁与伪变化抑制，并设计硬区域协同训练策略仅对低置信度像素进行监督。

Result: 在HZNU-FCD上达到97.63% F1、96.32% IoU、96.35% SCD_IoU_mean，仅6.65M可训练参数；相比ChangeCLIP-ViT提升10.19个百分点的F1；在LEVIR-CD和WHU-CD上也取得优异结果。

Conclusion: 所提方法在细粒度农田变化检测上表现优异，且具有强鲁棒性和泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Farmland Semantic Change Detection (SCD) is essential for cultivated land protection, yet existing benchmarks and models remain insufficient for fine-grained farmland conversion monitoring. Current datasets often lack dedicated "from-to" annotations, while visual change detection models are easily disturbed by phenology-induced pseudo-changes caused by crop rotation, seasonal variation, and illumination differences. To address these challenges, we construct HZNU-FCD, a large-scale fine-grained farmland SCD benchmark with a unified five-class farmland-to-non-farmland annotation protocol. It contains 4,588 bitemporal image pairs with pixel-level labels for practical farmland protection. Based on this benchmark, we propose a large-small collaborative SCD framework that integrates a task-driven small visual model with a frozen large vision-language model. The small model, Fine-grained Difference-aware Mamba (FD-Mamba), learns dense change representations for boundary preservation and small-region localization. The large-model pathway, Cross-modal Logical Arbitration (CMLA), introduces CLIP-based textual priors for prompt-guided semantic arbitration and pseudo-change suppression. To enable effective collaboration, we design a hard-region co-training strategy that supervises the CMLA semantic score map only on low-confidence pixels. Experiments show that our method achieves 97.63% F1, 96.32% IoU, and 96.35% SCD_IoU_mean on HZNU-FCD with only 6.65M trainable parameters. Compared with the multimodal ChangeCLIP-ViT, which leverages vision-language information for change detection, our method improves F1 by 10.19 percentage points on HZNU-FCD. It also achieves 91.43% F1 and 84.21% IoU on LEVIR-CD, and 93.85% F1 and 88.41% IoU on WHU-CD, demonstrating strong robustness and generalization. The code is available at https://github.com/Lovelymili/FD-Mamba.

</details>


### [113] [EgoEV-HandPose: Egocentric 3D Hand Pose Estimation and Gesture Recognition with Stereo Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12297)
*Luming Wang,Hao Shi,Jiajun Zhai,Kailun Yang,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoEV-HandPose框架，利用立体事件流实现双目3D手部姿态估计与手势识别，并发布首个大规模真实世界立体事件相机数据集EgoEVHands，在低光照和双手遮挡场景下显著优于RGB和现有事件相机方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧相机存在运动模糊和有限动态范围问题；现有事件相机方法受限于自运动干扰、单目深度模糊以及缺乏大规模真实世界立体数据集。

Method: 提出端到端框架EgoEV-HandPose，核心是KeypointBEV立体融合模块：将特征提升到规范鸟瞰图空间，并采用迭代重投影引导的精化循环逐步解决深度不确定性并强制执行运动学一致性约束。

Result: MPJPE 30.54mm，Top-1手势识别准确率86.87%，在低光照和双手遮挡场景下显著优于RGB立体方法和现有事件相机方法。

Conclusion: EgoEV-HandPose为基于事件的自我中心感知设立了新基准，EgoEVHands数据集将推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Egocentric 3D hand pose estimation and gesture recognition are essential for immersive augmented/virtual reality, human-computer interaction, and robotics. However, conventional frame-based cameras suffer from motion blur and limited dynamic range, while existing event-based methods are hindered by ego-motion interference, monocular depth ambiguity, and the lack of large-scale real-world stereo datasets. To overcome these limitations, we propose EgoEV-HandPose, an end-to-end framework for joint 3D bimanual pose estimation and gesture recognition from stereo event streams. Central to our approach is KeypointBEV, a flexible stereo fusion module that lifts features into a canonical bird's-eye-view space and employs an iterative reprojection-guided refinement loop to progressively resolve depth uncertainty and enforce kinematic consistency. In addition, we introduce EgoEVHands, the first large-scale real-world stereo event-camera dataset for egocentric hand perception, containing 5,419 annotated sequences with dense 3D/2D keypoints across 38 gesture classes under varying illumination. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EgoEV-HandPose achieves state-of-the-art performance with an MPJPE of 30.54mm and 86.87% Top-1 gesture recognition accuracy, significantly outperforming RGB-based stereo and prior event-camera methods, particularly in low-light and bimanual occlusion scenarios, thereby setting a new benchmark for event-based egocentric perception. The established dataset and source code will be publicly released at https://github.com/ZJUWang01/EgoEV-HandPose.

</details>


### [114] [Images in Sentences: Scaling Interleaved Instructions for Unified Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12305)
*Yabo Zhang,Kunchang Li,Dewei Zhou,Xinyu Huang,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: INSET通过将图像作为原生词汇嵌入文本指令中，利用Transformer的上下文局部性实现精确对象绑定，解决了多模态语言模型在处理复杂交错指令时的性能下降问题。该方法在15M高质量合成样本上训练，显著超越了现有SOTA方法，并能自然扩展到多模态图像编辑任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理复杂交错图文指令时性能下降，根本原因在于图像和文本的结构分离迫使模型建立困难的长距离依赖关系来匹配描述与视觉目标。

Method: 提出INSET统一生成模型，将图像作为原生词汇嵌入文本指令中；视觉特征直接放置于对应语义位置，利用Transformer的上下文局部性实现精确对象绑定；构建可扩展数据引擎，使用VLM和LLM从标准图像/视频数据集中合成15M高质量交错样本。

Result: 在InterleaveBench上显著优于现有SOTA方法，多图像一致性和文本对齐性能领先，且输入复杂度越高性能差距越明显；自然扩展到多模态图像编辑任务。

Conclusion: 将图像作为语言原生的嵌入方式是解决复杂交错图文生成任务的有效范式，且具有向多模态图像编辑等下游任务迁移的自然扩展性。

Abstract: While recent advancements in multimodal language models have enabled image generation from expressive multi-image instructions, existing methods struggle to maintain performance under complex interleaved instructions. This limitation stems from the structural separation of images and text in current paradigms, which forces models to bridge difficult long-range dependencies to match descriptions with visual targets. To address these challenges, we propose \texttt{I}mages i\texttt{N} \texttt{SE}n\texttt{T}ences (\textit{a.k.a}, INSET), a unified generation model that seamlessly embeds images as native vocabulary within textual instructions. By positioning visual features directly at their corresponding semantic slots, INSET leverages the contextual locality of transformers for precise object binding, effectively treating images as dense, expressive language tokens. Furthermore, we introduce a scalable data engine that synthesizes 15M high-quality interleaved samples from standard image and video datasets, utilizing VLMs and LLMs to construct rich, long-horizon sequences. Evaluation results on InterleaveBench demonstrate that INSET significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in multi-image consistency and text alignment, with performance gaps widening as input complexity increases. Beyond standard generation, our approach inherently extends to multimodal image editing, integrating visual content as part of the instruction to facilitate highly expressive and creative visual manipulations.

</details>


### [115] [G$^2$TR: Generation-Guided Visual Token Reduction for Separate-Encoder Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12309)
*Junxian Li,Kai Liu,Zizhong Ding,Zhixin Wang,Zhikai Chen,Renjing Pei,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出G²TR，一种面向分离编码器统一多模态模型（UMM）的生成引导视觉token精简框架。该方法利用生成分支提供的VAE潜空间一致性信号来评估视觉token重要性，通过平衡选择和冗余合并，在不进行训练的情况下实现1.94倍的视觉token和预填充计算量压缩，同时保持推理准确性和图像编辑质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离编码器UMMs因密集视觉token处理导致推理成本快速增长。现有视觉token精简方法多假设最终目标为判别式推理，不适用于需要同时保持图像编辑能力的UMMs。因此需要一种任务无关的token精简方法，既能保留语义相关性，又能维持隐空间图像重建与生成能力。

Method: G²TR包含三个步骤：1) 通过计算视觉token与VAE隐空间的一致性评估token重要性；2) 执行平衡的token选择；3) 将冗余token合并到保留的代表性token中以减少信息损失。该方法无需训练，即插即用，仅在理解编码阶段后应用。

Result: 在图像理解和编辑基准测试上，G²TR实现了1.94倍的视觉token和预填充计算量压缩，同时在推理准确性和编辑质量上均优于基线方法，几乎在所有基准上取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: G²TR有效解决了UMMs中理解端视觉token精简问题，通过生成引导的方式在保持生成能力的同时大幅提升推理效率，且无需额外训练即可适配现有推理管线。

Abstract: The development of separate-encoder Unified multimodal models (UMMs) comes with a rapidly growing inference cost due to dense visual token processing. In this paper, we focus on understanding-side visual token reduction for improving the efficiency of separate-encoder UMMs. While this topic has been widely studied for MLLMs, existing methods typically rely on attention scores, text-image similarity and so on, implicitly assuming that the final objective is discriminative reasoning. This assumption does not hold for UMMs, where understanding-side visual tokens must also preserve the model's capabilities for editing images. We propose G$^2$TR, a generation-guided visual token reduction framework for separate-encoder UMMs. Our key insight is that the generation branch provides a task-agnostic signal for identifying understanding-side visual tokens that are not only semantically relevant but also important for latent-space image reconstruction and generation. G$^2$TR estimates token importance from consistency with VAE latent, performs balanced token selection, and merges redundant tokens into retained representatives to reduce information loss. The method is training-free, plug-and-play, and applied only after the understanding encoding stage, making it compatible with existing UMM inference pipelines. Experiments on image understanding and editing benchmarks show that G$^2$TR substantially reduces visual tokens and prefill computation by 1.94x while maintaining both reasoning accuracy and editing quality, outperforming baselines on almost all benchmarks.

</details>


### [116] [Contrastive Learning under Noisy Temporal Self-Supervision for Colonoscopy Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12320)
*Luca Parolari,Pietro Gori,Lamberto Ballan,Carlo Biffi,Loic Le Folgoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时序结构的噪声感知对比学习方法，仅用27个视频训练轻量编码器，在息肉检索、重识别、尺寸估计和组织学分类等下游任务上超越自监督和 supervised 基线，匹配或超越基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督对比学习依赖正确的正负样本定义，而收集这些标签需要将视频中描述同一息肉的轨迹关联起来，成本高昂且需要临床专业知识。作者利用结肠镜操作的时间顺序结构来自监督地推导关联，以降低标注成本。

Method: 利用结肠镜操作的时间工作流从时间结构中推导自监督关联；由于时间推导的关联不一定正确，引入一种噪声感知的对比损失函数来处理噪声关联；使用轻量级编码器在仅27个视频上训练。

Result: 在息肉检索、重识别、尺寸估计和组织学分类等多个下游任务中，该方法超越了先前的自监督和监督基线，并在所有任务上匹配或超越了最新的基础模型。

Conclusion: 利用时序结构进行噪声感知对比学习，可以在极少量数据和轻量模型条件下取得与基础模型相当甚至更优的效果，验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Learning robust representations of polyp tracklets is key to enabling multiple AI-assisted colonoscopy applications, from polyp characterization to automated reporting and retrieval. Supervised contrastive learning is an effective approach for learning such representations, but it typically relies on correct positive and negative definitions. Collecting these labels requires linking tracklets that depict the same underlying polyp entity throughout the video, which is costly and demands specialized clinical expertise. In this work, we leverage the sequential workflow of colonoscopy procedures to derive self-supervised associations from temporal structure. Since temporally derived associations are not guaranteed to be correct, we introduce a noise-aware contrastive loss to account for noisy associations. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the learned representations across multiple downstream tasks, including polyp retrieval and re-identification, size estimation, and histology classification. Our method outperforms prior self-supervised and supervised baselines, and matches or exceeds recent foundation models across all tasks, using a lightweight encoder trained on only 27 videos. Code is available at https://github.com/lparolari/ntssl.

</details>


### [117] [VIP: Visual-guided Prompt Evolution for Efficient Dense Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12325)
*Hao Zhu,Shuo Jin,Wenbin Liao,Jiayu Xiao,Yan Zhu,Siyue Yu,Feng Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIP方法利用视觉引导的提示进化机制，在训练-free条件下提升了dino.txt框架的开放词汇语义分割性能，超越当前最优方法1.4%~8.4% mIoU，且计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的开放词汇语义分割方法存在深层空间偏差，且训练-free方案在效率和泛化性上仍面临挑战。作者旨在超越CLIP范式，利用dino.txt的空间感知能力，同时解决其文本查询语义模糊导致跨模态交互失配的问题。

Method: 提出视觉引导提示进化(VIP)方法，包含别名扩展(alias expansion)和视觉引导蒸馏机制，挖掘有价值的语义线索，并通过显著性感知聚合方式生成高保真预测结果，用于修正dino.txt中文本查询的语义表达。

Result: 在多个基准上超越顶级方法1.4%~8.4%平均mIoU，在多样化困难领域具有良好泛化性，且推理时间和内存开销极小。

Conclusion: VIP有效释放了dino.txt在细粒度目标感知方面的潜力，以高效、泛化的方式实现了无需训练的开放词汇语义分割，代码已开源。

Abstract: Pursuing training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation in an efficient and generalizable manner remains challenging due to the deep-seated spatial bias in CLIP. To overcome the limitations of existing solutions, this work moves beyond the CLIP-based paradigm and harnesses the recent spatially-aware dino.txt framework to facilitate more efficient and high-quality dense prediction. While dino.txt exhibits robust spatial awareness, we find that the semantic ambiguity of text queries gives rise to severe mismatch within its dense cross-modal interactions. To address this, we introduce \textcolor{oursblue}{\textbf{VI}}sual-guided \textcolor{oursblue}{\textbf{P}}rompt evolution (\textcolor{oursblue}{\textbf{\textit{VIP}}}) to rectify the semantic expressiveness of text queries in dino.txt, unleashing its potential for fine-grained object perception. Towards this end, \VIP integrates alias expansion with a visual-guided distillation mechanism to mine valuable semantic cues, which are robustly aggregated in a saliency-aware manner to yield a high-fidelity prediction. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that \VIP: \ding{182} surpasses the top-leading methods by $1.4\% \sim 8.4\%$ average mIoU, \ding{183} generalizes well to diverse challenging domains, and \ding{184} requires marginal inference time and memory overhead. \href{https://github.com/MiSsU-HH/VIP}{Our code is publicly available at GitHub \faGithub}.

</details>


### [118] [Fill the GAP: A Granular Alignment Paradigm for Visual Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12374)
*Yanting Miao,Yutao Sun,Dexin Wang,Mengyu Zhou,Pascal Poupart,Lei Lv,Qi Zhao,Li Wang,Hao Li,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP（粒度对齐范式），通过特征级、上下文级和能力引导三级对齐解决视觉潜在推理中预归一化MLLM的特征空间不匹配问题，在Qwen2.5-VL 7B上取得最优感知与推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉潜在推理方法在预归一化MLLM中存在特征空间不匹配问题：解码器隐藏状态与输入嵌入的归一化范数差异显著，导致直接潜在反馈不可靠，性能提升不稳定。

Method: 提出GAP三级对齐范式：(1) 特征级对齐：通过轻量级PCA对齐的潜在头将解码器输出映射为输入兼容的视觉潜在表示；(2) 上下文级对齐：利用可检查的辅助视觉监督锚定潜在目标；(3) 能力引导对齐：选择性地对基础MLLM难以处理的样本施加潜在监督。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL 7B上，GAP在有监督变体中取得了最优的平均聚合感知与推理性能。干预探测实验表明生成的潜在表示提供了任务相关的视觉信号，不仅仅增加了token槽位。

Conclusion: 通过多级对齐有效解决了特征空间不匹配问题，使视觉潜在推理变得稳定可靠，生成的潜在表示确实编码了任务相关的视觉信息。

Abstract: Visual latent reasoning lets a multimodal large language model (MLLM) create intermediate visual evidence as continuous tokens, avoiding external tools or image generators. However, existing methods usually follow an output-as-input latent paradigm and yield unstable gains. We identify evidence for a feature-space mismatch that can contribute to this instability: dominant visual-latent models build on pre-norm MLLMs and reuse decoder hidden states as predicted latent inputs, even though these states occupy a substantially different norm regime from the input embeddings the model was trained to consume~\citep{xie2025mhc,li2026siamesenorm,team2026attention}. This mismatch can make direct latent feedback unreliable. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose \textbf{GAP}, a \textbf{G}ranular \textbf{A}lignment \textbf{P}aradigm for visual latent modeling. GAP aligns visual latent reasoning at three levels: feature-level alignment maps decoder outputs into input-compatible visual latents through a lightweight PCA-aligned latent head; context-level alignment grounds latent targets with inspectable auxiliary visual supervision; and capacity-guided alignment assigns latent supervision selectively to examples where the base MLLM struggles. On Qwen2.5-VL 7B, the resulting model achieves the best mean aggregate perception and reasoning performance among our supervised variants. Inference-time intervention probing further suggests that generated latents provide task-relevant visual signal beyond merely adding token slots.

</details>


### [119] [Fast Image Super-Resolution via Consistency Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12377)
*Jiaqi Xu,Wenbo Li,Haoze Sun,Fan Li,Zhixin Wang,Long Peng,Jingjing Ren,Haoran Yang,Xiaowei Hu,Renjing Pei,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowSR提出将超分问题重定义为从低分辨率到高分辨率的整流流，通过改进的一致性学习策略实现单步高质量超分，并引入快慢调度策略平衡效率与细节保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在超分任务中虽然效果优异，但存在多步采样效率低下的问题；现有少步或单步方法要么对噪声输入建模效率低，要么未能充分利用迭代生成先验，导致重建图像的保真度和质量受损。

Method: 1) 将超分问题重定义为从LR到HR的整流流；2) 改进一致性蒸馏过程，引入HR正则化项确保学习到的SR流既保持自一致性又能精确收敛到真实HR目标；3) 提出快慢调度策略，从快调度器（少时间步）和慢调度器（多时间步）中分别采样相邻时间步用于一致性学习，以平衡效率和细粒度纹理细节的捕获。

Result: 大量实验表明FlowSR在效率和图像质量方面均实现了卓越的性能。

Conclusion: FlowSR通过整流流结合改进的一致性学习及快慢调度策略，有效解决了扩散模型超分任务中效率与质量之间的权衡问题，实现了单步高质量超分重建。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in real-world image super-resolution (SR), yet their reliance on time-consuming multi-step sampling largely hinders their practical applications. While recent efforts have introduced few- or single-step solutions, existing methods either inefficiently model the process from noisy input or fail to fully exploit iterative generative priors, compromising the fidelity and quality of the reconstructed images. To address this issue, we propose FlowSR, a novel approach that reformulates the SR problem as a rectified flow from low-resolution (LR) to high-resolution (HR) images. Our method leverages an improved consistency learning strategy to enable high-quality SR in a single step. Specifically, we refine the original consistency distillation process by incorporating HR regularization, ensuring that the learned SR flow not only enforces self-consistency but also converges precisely to the ground-truth HR target. Furthermore, we introduce a fast-slow scheduling strategy, where adjacent timesteps for consistency learning are sampled from two distinct schedulers: a fast scheduler with fewer timesteps to improve efficiency, and a slow scheduler with more timesteps to capture fine-grained texture details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlowSR achieves outstanding performance in both efficiency and image quality.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Localization: A Comprehensive Diagnosis of Perspective-Conditioned Spatial Reasoning in MLLMs from Omnidirectional Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12413)
*Yuangong Chen,Wai Keung Wong,Jiaxing Li,Ioannis Patras,Xu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PCSR-Bench基准测试，评估多模态大语言模型在360度全景图像中的视角条件空间推理能力。实验发现14个主流MLLMs存在显著的感知-推理鸿沟（从57.59%下降到0.64%），并通过强化学习诊断研究证明该能力具有部分可塑性但受任务选择性和奖励设计影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在视觉感知方面表现强劲，但在视角变化下的空间推理能力仍然有限。360度全景图像虽然提供了广泛场景覆盖，但仍需要视角依赖的推理，现有的基准测试缺乏对这一能力的系统性评估。

Method: 构建PCSR-Bench诊断基准，包含84,373个问答对、2,600张全景图像、26个室内环境，覆盖8个任务（基础感知任务如物体计数、相对距离、相对方向；高级PCSR任务如组合链、自我中心旋转、视角重锚定、自我中心失真、有限FOV可见性）。评估14个代表性MLLMs，并对7B规模模型进行基于强化学习的诊断研究。

Result: 14个MLLMs在基础相对方向任务上准确率达57.59%，但在自我中心旋转任务上降至13.49%，自我中心失真任务上降至7.13%，开放式组合推理任务上仅0.64%。强化学习训练可将7B基线模型从31.10%提升至60.06%，但增益具有任务选择性，对奖励设计敏感。

Conclusion: PCSR是当前MLLMs的关键瓶颈，该能力并非完全不可改变，具有部分可塑性，但提升空间有限且受任务类型、奖励设计和评估协议等多种因素制约。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong visual perception, yet remain limited in reasoning about space under changing viewpoints. We study this challenge as Perspective-Conditioned Spatial Reasoning (PCSR) in 360-degree omnidirectional images, where broad scene coverage reduces ambiguity from partial observations without eliminating the need for viewpoint-dependent inference. To assess this capability, we introduce PCSR-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 84,373 question-answer pairs from 2,600 omnidirectional images across 26 indoor environments. PCSR-Bench contains eight tasks spanning foundational perception (e.g., object counting, relative distance, and relative direction) and advanced PCSR, including compositional chains, egocentric rotation, perspective re-anchoring, ego-distortion, and limited-FOV visibility. We evaluate 14 representative MLLMs and observe a substantial perception-reasoning gap: accuracy reaches 57.59% on foundational relative direction, but drops to 13.49% on egocentric rotation, 7.13% on egocentric distortion, and 0.64% on open-ended compositional reasoning. To probe the plasticity of this gap, we conduct an RL-based diagnostic study on a 7B-scale model. Reward shaping improves a matched 7B baseline from 31.10% to 60.06% under a controlled setting, suggesting that PCSR is partial plasticity rather than being fully immutable. Still, the gains are task-selective, sensitive to reward design including both weight allocation and reward formulation, and partially dependent on the evaluation protocol. These results position PCSR as a key bottleneck in current MLLMs and highlight limited but meaningful room for recovery under targeted optimization.

</details>


### [121] [AOI-SSL: Self-Supervised Framework for Efficient Segmentation of Wire-bonded Semiconductors In Optical Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12430)
*Joaquín Figueira,Rob Van Gastel,Giacomo D'Amicantonio,Zhuoran Liu,Ioan Gabriel Bucur,Faysal Boughorbel,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AOI-SSL框架，结合小域自监督预训练（Masked Autoencoders最优）与上下文检索推理，实现线焊半导体图像的高效语义分割，在少量标注下显著优于从头训练和ImageNet预训练方法，且基于检索的分割在单设备图像上超越微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半导体自动光学检测（AOI）中的分割模型具有设备特异性，面临新设备或分布偏移时需重新训练，缺乏高效且泛化能力强的解决方案。

Method: 1）在小规模工业检测数据集上对Vision Transformer进行自监督预训练（对比MAE、DINO等），选择最优的MAE；2）提出上下文补丁级检索方法，直接从密集编码器嵌入预测分割掩码，无需大量额外训练；3）对比相似性检索与注意力聚合两种检索方式的效果。

Result: MAE在小数据自监督预训练中最优；自监督预训练显著优于从头训练和ImageNet预训练；基于检索的分割在单设备图像上优于微调，实现近即时适应；简单相似性检索与复杂注意力聚合表现相当。

Conclusion: AOI-SSL框架通过小域自监督预训练和上下文检索推理，有效降低了标注需求，提升了分割质量和对新设备的快速适应能力，为工业AOI提供了一种高效且泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Segmentation models in automated optical inspection of wire-bonded semiconductors are typically device-specific and must be re-trained when new devices or distribution shifts appear. We introduce AOI-SSL, a training-efficient framework for semantic segmentation of wire-bonded semiconductors by combining small-domain self-supervised pre-training of vision transformers with in-context inference that minimizes the need of labeled examples. We pre-train SOTA self-supervised algorithms in a small industrial inspection dataset and find that Masked Autoencoders are the most effective in this small-data setting, improving downstream segmentation while reducing the labeled fine-tuning effort. We further introduce in-context, patch-level retrieval methods that predict masks directly from dense encoder embeddings with negligible additional training. We show that, in this setting, simple similarity-based retrieval performs on par with more complex attention-based aggregation used currently in the literature. Furthermore, our experiments demonstrate that self-supervised pre-training significantly improves segmentation quality compared to training from scratch and to ImageNet pre-trained backbones under a fixed fine-tuning computational budget. Finally, the results reveal that retrieval based segmentation outperforms fine-tuning when targeting single device images, allowing for near-instant adaptation to difficult samples.

</details>


### [122] [GaitProtector: Impersonation-Driven Gait De-Identification via Training-Free Diffusion Latent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12431)
*Huiran Duan,Qian Zhou,Zhongliang Guo,Junhao Dong,Yuqi Li,Guoying Zhao,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaitProtector提出了一种基于模仿驱动的步态去身份化框架，通过将隐私保护统一为混淆和模仿两个目标，利用预训练3D扩散模型在无需重新训练的情况下对步态序列进行潜在优化，在有效抑制身份信息的同时保持步态结构的时空合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统步态去身份化方法存在身份抑制不足或引入时空失真、影响下游结构敏感应用的固有缺陷，亟需一种既能有效保护隐私又能保持步态结构合理性的方法。

Method: 提出GaitProtector框架，包含混淆（推离源身份）和模仿（拉向目标身份）两个耦合目标；利用预训练3D视频扩散模型，通过无需训练的潜在优化管线，将输入步态序列反转为潜在轨迹并迭代优化，以合成受保护的步态。

Result: 在CASIA-B数据集上，黑盒步态识别下模仿成功率达56.7%，Rank-1识别准确率从89.6%降至15.0%；在Scoliosis1K数据集上，诊断准确率仅从91.4%降至74.2%。

Conclusion: 本文首次以无需训练的方式利用预训练3D扩散先验进行基于剪影的步态去身份化，在有效去身份的同时保持了步态的视觉质量和时空一致性。

Abstract: Conventional gait de-identification methods often encounter an inherent trade-off: they either provide insufficient identity suppression or introduce spatiotemporal distortions that impede structure-sensitive downstream applications. We propose GaitProtector, an impersonation-driven gait de-identification framework that formulates privacy protection as a unified objective with two tightly coupled components: (i) obfuscation, which repels the protected gait from the source identity, and (ii) impersonation, which attracts it toward a selected target identity. The target identity serves as a semantic anchor that biases optimization toward structurally plausible gait patterns under the pretrained diffusion prior, helping preserve dominant body shape and motion dynamics. We instantiate this idea through a training-free diffusion latent optimization pipeline. Instead of retraining a generator for each dataset, we invert each input silhouette sequence into the latent trajectory of a pretrained 3D video diffusion model and iteratively optimize latent codes with a differentiable adversarial objective to synthesize protected gaits. Experiments on the CASIA-B dataset show that GaitProtector achieves a 56.7% impersonation success rate under black-box gait recognition and reduces Rank-1 identification accuracy from 89.6% to 15.0%, while maintaining favorable visual and temporal quality. We further evaluate downstream utility on the Scoliosis1K dataset, where diagnostic accuracy decreases only from 91.4% to 74.2%. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to leverage pretrained 3D diffusion priors in a training-free manner for silhouette-based gait de-identification.

</details>


### [123] [3D Gaussian Splatting for Efficient Retrospective Dynamic Scene Novel View Synthesis with a Standardized Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12437)
*Yunxiao Zhang,Suryansh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在同步多视角设置下，无需显式时间变形约束即可实现高效的动态场景回溯式新视角合成，并配套开源了一个基于Blender的动态多视角数据集生成框架以支持可复现的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3DGS方法普遍引入时间耦合或复杂多体约束来强制运动一致性，但在同步多视角（如体育场景）中，每个时刻的动态场景已受到极强的几何约束。作者质疑上述额外约束的必要性，希望探索更简洁高效的方案。

Method: 在起始时刻从SfM点云初始化，随后随时间传播优化后的3D高斯体，不施加任何时间变形约束；同时构建了一个基于Blender的动态多视角数据集框架，生成高质量同步相机阵列并导出标准格式训练数据。

Result: 在同步多视角设置下，无需时间变形约束即可实现高效的回溯式动态场景新视角合成；所提数据集框架消除了坐标约定和数据管线的不一致性，支持可控条件下的可复现基准评测。

Conclusion: 同步多视角提供的空间一致性足以支撑动态场景的重建，显式时间耦合并非必要；所提出的数据集框架为动态NVS方法提供了标准化、可复现的评测平台。

Abstract: Retrospective novel view synthesis (NVS) of dynamic scenes is fundamental to applications such as sports. Recent dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) approaches introduce temporally coupled formulations to enforce motion coherence across time. In this paper, we argue that, in a synchronized multi-view (MV) setting typical of sports, the dynamic scene at each time step is already strongly geometrically constrained. We posit that the availability of calibrated, synchronized viewpoints provides sufficient spatial consistency, and therefore, explicit temporal coupling, or complex multi-body constraints seems unnecessary for retrospective NVS. To this end, we propose an approach tailored for synchronized MV dynamic scene. By initializing the SfM-derived point cloud at the start time and propagating optimized Gaussians over time, we show that efficient retrospective NVS can be achieved without imposing a temporal deformation constraint. Complementing our methodological contribution, we introduce a Dynamic MV dataset framework built on Blender for reproducible NeRF and 3DGS research. The framework generates high-quality, synchronized camera rigs and exports training-ready datasets in standard formats, eliminating inconsistencies in coordinate conventions and data pipelines. Using the framework, we construct a dynamic benchmark suite and evaluate representative NeRF and 3DGS approaches under controlled conditions. Together, we show that, under a synchronized MV setup, efficient retrospective dynamic scene NVS can be achieved using 3DGS. At the same time, the dataset-generation framework enables reproducible and principled benchmarking of dynamic NVS methods.

</details>


### [124] [LychSim: A Controllable and Interactive Simulation Framework for Vision Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12449)
*Wufei Ma,Chloe Wang,Siyi Chen,Jiawei Peng,Patrick Li,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LychSim 是一个基于 Unreal Engine 5 构建的高可控、交互式仿真框架，通过简洁的 Python API、程序化数据流水线和 MCP 协议集成，降低了视觉仿真技术门槛，支持合成数据生成、强化学习对抗测试和语言驱动场景布局等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台技术门槛高，需要计算机图形学和游戏开发的专业知识；而自监督预训练虽减少了对合成数据的依赖，但仿真在闭环优化和 OOD 评估中仍不可或缺，因此需要更易用的仿真框架。

Method: 基于 Unreal Engine 5 构建，核心设计包括：(1) 简洁的 Python API 屏蔽底层引擎复杂度；(2) 程序化数据流水线生成多样化高保真 OOD 场景及丰富 2D/3D 真值；(3) 集成 Model Context Protocol (MCP)，支持推理型 LLM 的闭环交互；同时标注场景级程序化规则和物体级位姿对齐以支持语义对齐的 3D 真值和自动场景修改。

Result: LychSim 在多个下游应用中展示了能力：作为合成数据引擎、支持强化学习驱动的对抗性测试、以及交互式语言驱动的场景布局生成。框架将公开完整源代码和数据标注。

Conclusion: LychSim 通过降低视觉仿真技术门槛，为计算机视觉社区提供了一个高可控、可交互的仿真平台，在合成数据生成、OOD 评估和 LLM 闭环推理等方面具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: While self-supervised pretraining has reduced vision systems' reliance on synthetic data, simulation remains an indispensable tool for closed-loop optimization and rigorous out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluation. However, modern simulation platforms often present steep technical barriers, requiring extensive expertise in computer graphics and game development. In this work, we present LychSim, a highly controllable and interactive simulation framework built upon Unreal Engine 5 to bridge this gap. LychSim is built around three key designs: (1) a streamlined Python API that abstracts away underlying engine complexities; (2) a procedural data pipeline capable of generating diverse, high-fidelity environments with varying out-of-distribution (OOD) visual challenges, paired with rich 2D and 3D ground truths; and (3) a native integration of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) that transforms the simulator into a dynamic, closed-loop playground for reasoning agentic LLMs. We further annotate scene-level procedural rules and object-level pose alignments to enable semantically aligned 3D ground truths and automated scene modification. We demonstrate LychSim's capability across multiple downstream applications, including serving as a synthetic data engine, powering reinforcement learning-based adversarial examiners, and facilitating interactive, language-driven scene layout generation. To benefit the broader vision community, LychSim will be made publicly available, including full source code and various data annotations.

</details>


### [125] [FuTCR: Future-Targeted Contrast and Repulsion for Continual Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12451)
*Nicholas Ikechukwu,Keanu Nichols,Deepti Ghadiyaram,Bryan A. Plummer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FuTCR框架，通过发现未来类区域、像素-区域对比和特征排斥机制，在连续全景分割中为新类别预留表示空间，在新类全景质量上提升高达28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS方法将未标记像素统一归为"背景"类，导致模型忽略不同背景类别间的差异，阻碍后续新类别的学习。

Method: 1) 发现置信的类未来区域：分组预测掩码中背景但具有非背景logits的像素；2) 像素-区域对比：构建未标注区域的一致原型；3) 特征排斥：将背景特征推离已知类原型，为新类别显式预留表示空间。

Result: 在六个CPS设置和多种数据集规模下，新类全景质量相对提升高达28%，基类性能提升达4%。

Conclusion: FuTCR通过在新类别引入前重构特征表示空间，有效解决了连续全景分割中背景类别混淆的问题。

Abstract: Continual Panoptic Segmentation (CPS) requires methods that can quickly adapt to new categories over time. The nature of this dense prediction task means that training images may contain a mix of labeled and unlabeled objects. As nothing is known about these unlabeled objects a priori, existing methods often simply group any unlabeled pixel into a single "background" class during training. In effect, during training, they repeatedly tell the model that all the different background categories are the same (even when they aren't). This makes learning to identify different background categories as they are added challenging since these new categories may require using information the model was previously told was unimportant and ignored. Thus, we propose a Future-Targeted Contrastive and Repulsive (FuTCR) framework that addresses this limitation by restructuring representations before new classes are introduced. FuTCR first discovers confident future-like regions by grouping model-predicted masks whose pixels are consistently classified as background but exhibit non-background logits. Next, FuTCR applies pixel-to-region contrast to build coherent prototypes from these unlabeled regions, while simultaneously repelling background features away from known-class prototypes to explicitly reserve representational space for future categories. Experiments across six CPS settings and a range of dataset sizes show FuTCR improves relative new-class panoptic quality over the state-of-the-art by up to 28%, while preserving or improving base-class performance with gains up to 4%.

</details>


### [126] [Elastic Attention Cores for Scalable Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12491)
*Alan Z. Song,Yinjie Chen,Mu Nan,Rui Zhang,Jiahang Cao,Weijian Mai,Muquan Yu,Hossein Adeli,Deva Ramanan,Michael J. Tarr,Andrew F. Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VECA提出了一种线性复杂度的核心-外围注意力机制，通过一组可学习的核心令牌实现图像块间的间接信息交换，在保持竞争性能的同时将计算复杂度从二次降至线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ViT的全对全自注意力计算复杂度随图像分辨率平方级增长，限制了高分辨率应用。本文挑战了"成对令牌交互对学习丰富视觉语义表示必要"的假设。

Method: 提出VECA架构，使用少量可学习的核心令牌作为通信接口，图像块令牌仅通过核心令牌间接交换信息。N个图像块与C个核心交互（C固定），实现O(N)线性复杂度。采用核心轴嵌套训练，支持推理时弹性调整计算量与精度。

Result: 在分类和密集预测任务上，VECA达到了与最新视觉基础模型竞争的性能，同时显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 弹性核心-外围注意力可以作为ViT的可扩展替代构建模块，证明了无需直接补丁间交互也能学习有效视觉表示。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong data-driven scaling by leveraging all-to-all self-attention. However, this flexibility incurs a computational cost that scales quadratically with image resolution, limiting ViTs in high-resolution domains. Underlying this approach is the assumption that pairwise token interactions are necessary for learning rich visual-semantic representations. In this work, we challenge this assumption, demonstrating that effective visual representations can be learned without any direct patch-to-patch interaction. We propose VECA (Visual Elastic Core Attention), a vision transformer architecture that uses efficient linear-time core-periphery structured attention enabled by a small set of learned cores. In VECA, these cores act as a communication interface: patch tokens exchange information exclusively through the core tokens, which are initialized from scratch and propagated across layers. Because the $N$ image patches only directly interact with a resolution invariant set of $C$ learned "core" embeddings, this yields linear complexity $O(N)$ for predetermined $C$, which bypasses quadratic scaling. Compared to prior cross-attention architectures, VECA maintains and iteratively updates the full set of $N$ input tokens, avoiding a small $C$-way bottleneck. Combined with nested training along the core axis, our model can elastically trade off compute and accuracy during inference. Across classification and dense tasks, VECA achieves performance competitive with the latest vision foundation models while reducing computational cost. Our results establish elastic core-periphery attention as a scalable alternative building block for Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [127] [Revisiting Photometric Ambiguity for Accurate Gaussian-Splatting Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12494)
*Jiahe Li,Jiawei Zhang,Xiao Bai,Jin Zheng,Xiaohan Yu,Lin Gu,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AmbiSuR框架，基于高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）解决光度模糊导致的表面重建问题，通过光度解模糊和模糊指示模块，在各种挑战场景下实现了优于现有方法的表面三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可微渲染的表面重建方法受限于光度模糊（photometric ambiguities），导致几何解不明确，重建质量存在瓶颈。

Method: 首先重新审视高斯泼溅表示，发现其中存在两种原语级别的固有权衡模糊性，并揭示其内在的模糊自指示潜力。基于此，提出光度解模糊（photometric disambiguation）约束不适定几何解；再设计模糊指示模块（ambiguity indication module），利用自指示潜力识别并引导修正欠约束重建。

Result: 在多种挑战场景下，AmbiSuR的表面重建质量优于现有方法，且具有广泛的兼容性。

Conclusion: AmbiSuR通过挖掘高斯泼溅的自指示潜力解决光度模糊问题，实现了高鲁棒性的表面三维重建，为后续研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Surface reconstruction with differentiable rendering has achieved impressive performance in recent years, yet the pervasive photometric ambiguities have strictly bottlenecked existing approaches. This paper presents AmbiSuR, a framework that explores an intrinsic solution upon Gaussian Splatting for the photometric ambiguity-robust surface 3D reconstruction with high performance. Starting by revisiting the foundation, our investigation uncovers two built-in primitive-wise ambiguities in representation, while revealing an intrinsic potential for ambiguity self-indication in Gaussian Splatting. Stemming from these, a photometric disambiguation is first introduced, constraining ill-posed geometry solution for definite surface formation. Then, we propose an ambiguity indication module that unleashes the self-indication potential to identify and further guide correcting underconstrained reconstructions. Extensive experiments demonstrate our superior surface reconstructions compared to existing methods across various challenging scenarios, excelling in broad compatibility. Project: https://fictionarry.github.io/AmbiSuR-Proj/ .

</details>


### [128] [AlphaGRPO: Unlocking Self-Reflective Multimodal Generation in UMMs via Decompositional Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12495)
*Runhui Huang,Jie Wu,Rui Yang,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaGRPO框架，将GRPO应用于多模态生成模型，无需冷启动阶段即可增强生成能力；引入DVReward奖励机制，利用LLM分解复杂请求为可验证问题，由MLLM评估，实现自我反思式强化学习，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生成模型缺乏自我反思和推理能力，且难以在真实多模态生成场景中提供稳定有效的监督信号。

Method: 将GRPO应用于AR-Diffusion统一多模态模型（UMMs）；提出Decompositional Verifiable Reward (DVReward)，利用LLM将复杂请求分解为原子化可验证问题，再由通用MLLM进行评估和反馈。

Result: 在GenEval、TIIF-Bench、DPG-Bench、WISE等多模态生成基准上取得显著提升；在GEdit编辑任务上未经训练也获得明显改进。

Conclusion: AlphaGRPO无需冷启动阶段即可激发模型内在推理潜力；DVReward提供了可靠可解释的奖励信号；自我反思式强化学习能有效利用模型自身理解能力指导高质量生成。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose AlphaGRPO, a novel framework that applies Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to AR-Diffusion Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) to enhance multimodal generation capabilities without an additional cold-start stage. Our approach unlocks the model's intrinsic potential to perform advanced reasoning tasks: Reasoning Text-to-Image Generation, where the model actively infers implicit user intents, and Self-Reflective Refinement, where it autonomously diagnoses and corrects misalignments in generated outputs. To address the challenge of providing stable supervision for real-world multimodal generation, we introduce the Decompositional Verifiable Reward (DVReward). Unlike holistic scalar rewards, DVReward utilizes an LLM to decompose complex user requests into atomic, verifiable semantic and quality questions, which are then evaluated by a general MLLM to provide reliable and interpretable feedback. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AlphaGRPO yields robust improvements across multimodal generation benchmarks, including GenEval, TIIF-Bench, DPG-Bench and WISE, while also achieving significant gains in editing tasks on GEdit without training on editing tasks. These results validate that our self-reflective reinforcement approach effectively leverages inherent understanding to guide high-fidelity generation. Project page: https://huangrh99.github.io/AlphaGRPO/

</details>


### [129] [CausalCine: Real-Time Autoregressive Generation for Multi-Shot Video Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12496)
*Yihao Meng,Zichen Liu,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Ka Leong Cheng,Yue Yu,Hanlin Wang,Haobo Li,Jiapeng Zhu,Yanhong Zeng,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Qifeng Chen,Huamin Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CausalCine 是一个交互式自回归视频生成框架，通过因果建模、内容感知记忆路由和少步蒸馏，实现了多镜头、可实时交互的长视频生成，在保持因果生成流式交互性的同时接近双向模型的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频模型主要针对短时延连续生成，处理长序列时会将多个镜头视为单个镜头的简单延长，导致运动停滞和语义漂移，无法实现叙事所需的镜头切换、视角变化和事件演进。

Method: 1) 在多镜头序列上训练因果基座模型以学习镜头转换先验；2) 提出内容感知记忆路由（CAMR），基于注意力相关性分数而非时间邻近性动态检索历史KV条目，在有界活动记忆下保持跨镜头连贯性；3) 将因果基座模型蒸馏为少步生成器以实现实时交互生成。

Result: CausalCine 显著优于自回归基线方法，在保持因果生成流式交互性的同时，性能接近双向模型。

Conclusion: CausalCine 将多镜头视频生成转化为在线导演过程，支持跨镜头因果生成、动态提示输入和上下文重用，无需重新生成先前镜头。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation aims at real-time, open-ended synthesis. Yet, cinematic storytelling is not merely the endless extension of a single scene; it requires progressing through evolving events, viewpoint shifts, and discrete shot boundaries. Existing autoregressive models often struggle in this setting. Trained primarily for short-horizon continuation, they treat long sequences as extended single shots, inevitably suffering from motion stagnation and semantic drift during long rollouts. To bridge this gap, we introduce CausalCine, an interactive autoregressive framework that transforms multi-shot video generation into an online directing process. CausalCine generates causally across shot changes, accepts dynamic prompts on the fly, and reuses context without regenerating previous shots. To achieve this, we first train a causal base model on native multi-shot sequences to learn complex shot transitions prior to acceleration. We then propose Content-Aware Memory Routing (CAMR), which dynamically retrieves historical KV entries according to attention-based relevance scores rather than temporal proximity, preserving cross-shot coherence under bounded active memory. Finally, we distill the causal base model into a few-step generator for real-time interactive generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalCine significantly outperforms autoregressive baselines and approaches the capability of bidirectional models while unlocking the streaming interactivity of causal generation. Demo available at https://yihao-meng.github.io/CausalCine/

</details>


### [130] [From Web to Pixels: Bringing Agentic Search into Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12497)
*Bokang Yang,Xinyi Sun,Kaituo Feng,Xingping Dong,Dongming Wu,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出感知深度研究（Perception Deep Research）这一新挑战，并构建了WebEye基准（含120张图像、473个标注实例、645个QA对、1927个任务样本），涵盖搜索式定位、分割和VQA三个任务视角。同时提出Pixel-Searcher智能搜索到像素的工作流，在开放世界场景中通过外部事实解析隐藏目标身份并完成定位，实验表明其达到开源最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉感知研究假设识别目标的关键证据已存在于图像或模型冻结知识中，但实际开放世界场景中，目标需先通过外部事实、近期事件、长尾实体或多跳关系解析才能定位。现有基准无法评估这种知识驱动的感知能力。

Method: 构建WebEye基准（目标锚定的可验证证据、知识密集型查询、精确框/掩码标注），定义搜索式定位/分割/VQA三种任务视角；提出Pixel-Searcher智能体搜索到像素工作流，将隐藏目标身份解析与框/掩码/接地答案绑定。

Result: Pixel-Searcher在三种任务视角上均达到开源最优性能；失败主要源于证据获取、身份解析和视觉实例绑定三个环节。

Conclusion: 感知深度研究是更具挑战性的开放世界视觉感知范式，Pixel-Searcher展示了搜索驱动感知的有效性，但证据获取、身份解析和实例绑定仍是主要瓶颈，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Visual perception connects high-level semantic understanding to pixel-level perception, but most existing settings assume that the decisive evidence for identifying a target is already in the image or frozen model knowledge. We study a more practical yet harder open-world case where a visible object must first be resolved from external facts, recent events, long-tail entities, or multi-hop relations before it can be localized. We formalize this challenge as Perception Deep Research and introduce WebEye, an object-anchored benchmark with verifiable evidence, knowledge-intensive queries, precise box/mask annotations, and three task views: Search-based Grounding, Search-based Segmentation, and Search-based VQA. WebEyes contains 120 images, 473 annotated object instances, 645 unique QA pairs, and 1,927 task samples. We further propose Pixel-Searcher, an agentic search-to-pixel workflow that resolves hidden target identities and binds them to boxes, masks, or grounded answers. Experiments show that Pixel-Searcher achieves the strongest open-source performance across all three task views, while failures mainly arise from evidence acquisition, identity resolution, and visual instance binding.

</details>


### [131] [EgoForce: Forearm-Guided Camera-Space 3D Hand Pose from a Monocular Egocentric Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12498)
*Christen Millerdurai,Shaoxiang Wang,Yaxu Xie,Vladislav Golyanik,Didier Stricker,Alain Pagani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoForce是一个单目3D手部重建框架，通过结合可微分前臂表示、统一手臂-手部变换器和射线空间闭式求解器，能够在鱼眼、透视和广角等多种头戴相机模型下，从用户视角鲁棒地恢复绝对3D手部姿态和位置，在HOT3D数据集上实现SOTA性能，相机空间MPJPE降低高达28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目RGB手部重建方法受限于深度-尺度模糊性，难以泛化到头戴设备多样化的光学配置，通常需要昂贵且耗时的设备特定数据集训练。

Method: 1) 可微分前臂表示（稳定手部姿态）；2) 统一手臂-手部变换器（从单目视图联合预测手部和前臂几何，缓解深度-尺度模糊性）；3) 射线空间闭式求解器（实现跨多种头戴相机模型的绝对3D姿态恢复）。

Result: 在三个自我中心基准数据集上达到SOTA；在HOT3D数据集上相机空间MPJPE相比先前方法降低28%；在多种相机配置下保持一致性表现。

Conclusion: EgoForce是一种适用于AR/VR等自我中心交互场景的鲁棒、通用且绝对尺度的3D手部重建方案，无需设备特定训练数据。

Abstract: Reconstructing the absolute 3D pose and shape of the hands from the user's viewpoint using a single head-mounted camera is crucial for practical egocentric interaction in AR/VR, telepresence, and hand-centric manipulation tasks, where sensing must remain compact and unobtrusive. While monocular RGB methods have made progress, they remain constrained by depth-scale ambiguity and struggle to generalize across the diverse optical configurations of head-mounted devices. As a result, models typically require extensive training on device-specific datasets, which are costly and laborious to acquire. This paper addresses these challenges by introducing EgoForce, a monocular 3D hand reconstruction framework that recovers robust, absolute 3D hand pose and its position from the user's (camera-space) viewpoint. EgoForce operates across fisheye, perspective, and distorted wide-FOV camera models using a single unified network. Our approach combines a differentiable forearm representation that stabilizes hand pose, a unified arm-hand transformer that predicts both hand and forearm geometry from a single egocentric view, mitigating depth-scale ambiguity, and a ray space closed-form solver that enables absolute 3D pose recovery across diverse head-mounted camera models. Experiments on three egocentric benchmarks show that EgoForce achieves state-of-the-art 3D accuracy, reducing camera-space MPJPE by up to 28% on the HOT3D dataset compared to prior methods and maintaining consistent performance across camera configurations. For more details, visit the project page at https://dfki-av.github.io/EgoForce.

</details>


### [132] [SenseNova-U1: Unifying Multimodal Understanding and Generation with NEO-unify Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12500)
*Haiwen Diao,Penghao Wu,Hanming Deng,Jiahao Wang,Shihao Bai,Silei Wu,Weichen Fan,Wenjie Ye,Wenwen Tong,Xiangyu Fan,Yan Li,Yubo Wang,Zhijie Cao,Zhiqian Lin,Zhitao Yang,Zhongang Cai,Yuwei Niu,Yue Zhu,Bo Liu,Chengguang Lv,Haojia Yu,Haozhe Xie,Hongli Wang,Jianan Fan,Jiaqi Li,Jiefan Lu,Jingcheng Ni,Junxiang Xu,Kaihuan Liang,Lianqiang Shi,Linjun Dai,Linyan Wang,Oscar Qian,Peng Gao,Pengfei Liu,Qingping Sun,Rui Shen,Ruisi Wang,Shengnan Ma,Shuang Yang,Siyi Xie,Siying Li,Tianbo Zhong,Xiangli Kong,Xuanke Shi,Yang Gao,Yongqiang Yao,Yves Wang,Zhengqi Bai,Zhengyu Lin,Zixin Yin,Wenxiu Sun,Ruihao Gong,Quan Wang,Lewei Lu,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseNova-U1提出了一种原生统一的多模态范式，打破视觉语言模型中理解与生成的二元对立，通过NEO-unify框架将两者融合为单一过程的协同视图，在多项任务上达到顶尖性能，并初步验证了在VLA和世界模型场景中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型视觉语言模型存在理解与生成割裂的结构性局限，这种二分法导致架构碎片化、级联管道和表征空间错位，阻碍了原生多模态智能的出现。

Method: 基于NEO-unify框架构建原生统一多模态范式，将理解与生成为单一底层过程的协同视图；在密集模型（8B）和混合专家模型（30B-A3B）基础上分别构建SenseNova-U1-8B-MoT和SenseNova-U1-A3B-MoT，并从模型设计、数据预处理、预/后训练及推理策略等方面提供详细方案。

Result: 在文本理解、视觉语言感知、知识推理、智能体决策、空间智能等方面媲美顶尖的纯理解VLM；在传统/知识密集型任意图生成、复杂文本丰富信息图生成、交错视觉语言生成中展现强语义一致性和视觉保真度；在视觉-语言-动作和世界模型场景中表现良好。

Conclusion: 多模态AI不应是连接独立系统的拼接，而应构建统一模型，让必要能力从内部涌现。SenseNova-U1朝这一方向迈出了实质性一步。

Abstract: Recent large vision-language models (VLMs) remain fundamentally constrained by a persistent dichotomy: understanding and generation are treated as distinct problems, leading to fragmented architectures, cascaded pipelines, and misaligned representation spaces. We argue that this divide is not merely an engineering artifact, but a structural limitation that hinders the emergence of native multimodal intelligence. Hence, we introduce SenseNova-U1, a native unified multimodal paradigm built upon NEO-unify, in which understanding and generation evolve as synergistic views of a single underlying process. We launch two native unified variants, SenseNova-U1-8B-MoT and SenseNova-U1-A3B-MoT, built on dense (8B) and mixture-of-experts (30B-A3B) understanding baselines, respectively. Designed from first principles, they rival top-tier understanding-only VLMs across text understanding, vision-language perception, knowledge reasoning, agentic decision-making, and spatial intelligence. Meanwhile, they deliver strong semantic consistency and visual fidelity, excelling in conventional or knowledge-intensive any-to-image (X2I) synthesis, complex text-rich infographic generation, and interleaved vision-language generation, with or without think patterns. Beyond performance, we show detailed model design, data preprocessing, pre-/post-training, and inference strategies to support community research. Last but not least, preliminary evidence demonstrates that our models extend beyond perception and generation, performing strongly in vision-language-action (VLA) and world model (WM) scenarios. This points toward a broader roadmap where models do not translate between modalities, but think and act across them in a native manner. Multimodal AI is no longer about connecting separate systems, but about building a unified one and trusting the necessary capabilities to emerge from within.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Sampling More, Getting Less: Calibration is the Diversity Bottleneck in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11128)
*Amin Banayeeanzade,Qingchuan Yang,Dhruv Tarsadiya,Fatemeh Bahrani,Leonardo Blas,Alfy Samuel,Robin Jia,Meisam Razaviyayn,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个有效性-多样性框架，将LLM的多样性崩溃归因于解码过程中概率分布的顺序校准和形状校准问题，并通过实验证明这是模型分布本身的缺陷而非采样策略的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽已开发出衡量LLM输出多样性不足的基准，但对推理时逐步概率分布如何导致多样性崩溃的内在机制理解不足。

Method: 提出有效性-多样性框架，将多样性瓶颈分解为两种校准问题：顺序校准（有效token未可靠地排在无效token之上）和形状校准（概率质量过度集中在少数有效token上，同时存在包含混合token的重尾分布）。通过已知有效集和oracle截断基线等受控诊断任务进行实证分析。

Result: 在14个不同规模和系列的语言模型上，发现多样性崩溃不仅是特定采样启发式方法的局限，而是LLM分布中顺序校准和形状校准缺陷的直接后果。局部失败会在解码步骤中累积，导致序列层面严重的多样性损失。

Conclusion: 多样性崩溃源于模型概率分布本身的结构性校准问题，而非单纯采样策略选择不当。这为改进LLM解码过程提供了更根本的理论视角和诊断工具。

Abstract: Diversity is essential for language-model applications ranging from creative generation to scientific discovery, yet modern LLMs often collapse into a narrow subset of plausible outputs. While prior work has developed benchmarks for measuring this lack of diversity, less is known about how the step-by-step probability distributions at inference time cause the problem. We introduce a validity--diversity framework that attributes diversity collapse to how an LLM allocates probability mass across valid and invalid continuations during decoding. This framework decomposes the bottleneck into two complementary forms of miscalibration. First, order calibration: valid tokens are not reliably ranked above invalid tokens, so rank-based cutoff rules must trade off between recovering valid continuations and admitting invalid ones. Second, shape calibration: probability mass is overly concentrated only on few valid continuations while having a heavy-tail of mixed valid and invalid tokens, so maintaining high validity limits diversity. We formalize both mechanisms and show that local failures compound across decoding steps, producing strong sequence-level losses in diversity. Empirically, we develop controlled diagnostics for probing these bottlenecks, including tasks with exactly known valid sets and oracle cutoff baselines. Across 14 language models spanning multiple families and scales, we find that diversity collapse is not merely a limitation of particular sampling heuristics, but a consequence of order and shape miscalibration in the LLM distribution.

</details>


### [134] [ClinicalBench: Stress-Testing Assertion-Aware Retrieval for Cross-Admission Clinical QA on MIMIC-IV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11143)
*Alex Stinard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EpiKG，一种结合断言标签和时间标签的患者知识图谱检索增强生成方法，在临床推理前置的检索环节上，通过意图感知路由显著提升EHR笔记检索准确率。在ClinicalBench（43名MIMIC-IV患者、400道断言敏感问题）上，经过两名外部医师盲审的50项严格一致子集上，比基线提升+22.0个百分点（p=0.0192）。同时发现56%的自动生成参考答案存在缺陷，表明临床QA基准必须经过医师裁定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床推理基准仅评估模型在干净输入上的表现，忽略了检索环节中否定、时序、家族史与患者归属等断言敏感因素对答案的颠覆性影响，因此需要专门评估和优化EHR检索环节。

Method: 构建EpiKG，为患者知识图谱中每个事实附加断言标签和时序标签，并根据问题意图进行路由检索。创建包含400个问题的ClinicalBench基准，覆盖9类断言敏感类别。采用7条件消融实验，在6个LLM上进行评估，并设计作者盲法、两位外部医师裁定为主要终点。

Result: 主要终点（50项严格一致项目）提升+22.0个百分点（95% CI [+5.1, +31.5], p=0.0192）；架构新颖性（意图感知KG-RAG vs Contriever密集RAG基线）提升+8.84个百分点（p=1.79e-3）；56%的自动生成参考答案被医师判定有缺陷。

Conclusion: EpiKG的意图感知KG-RAG方法在断言敏感的临床检索任务上显著优于基线；收益随LLM自身能力提升而递减（beta=-1.123, r=-0.921, p=0.009），但更像回归均值而非编码替代模型规模；临床QA基准必须经过医师裁定；资源已公开释放。

Abstract: Reasoning benchmarks measure clinical performance on clean inputs. We evaluate the step before reasoning: retrieval over real EHR notes, where negation, temporality, and family-versus-patient attribution can flip a correct answer to a wrong one. EpiKG carries an assertion label and a temporality tag with every fact in a patient knowledge graph, then routes retrieval by question intent. ClinicalBench is a 400-question test over 43 MIMIC-IV patients across 9 assertion-sensitive categories. A 7-condition ablation tests each piece of EpiKG across six LLMs (Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-OSS 20B, MedGemma 27B, Gemma 4 31B, MedGemma 1.5 4B, Qwen 3.5 35B). Three physicians blindly adjudicated 100 paired items. The author-blind primary endpoint, leave-author-out paired exact McNemar on 50 unanimous-strict items rated by two external physicians, yields +22.0 percentage points (95 percent Newcombe CI [+5.1, +31.5], p=0.0192). The architectural novelty, intent-aware KG-RAG over a Contriever dense-RAG baseline (C2b to C4g_kw on the change-excluded n=362 endpoint), is +8.84 percentage points (paired McNemar p=1.79e-3); +12.43 percentage points under oracle intent. Sensitivities agree directionally: three-rater physician majority +24.0 percentage points (subject to single-author circularity); deterministic keyword reproducibility proxy +39.5 percentage points. Across the six models, the gain shrinks as the LLM-alone baseline rises (beta=-1.123, r=-0.921, p=0.009). With n=6 this looks more like regression to the mean than encoding substituting for model size. Physician adjudication identified 56 percent of auto-generated reference answers as defective, a methodological finding indicating that NLP-pipeline clinical-QA benchmarks require physician adjudication to be usable. ClinicalBench, the frozen evaluator, three-rater adjudication data, and the EpiKG output stack are publicly released.

</details>


### [135] [Decomposing Evolutionary Mixture-of-LoRA Architectures: The Routing Lever, the Lifecycle Penalty, and a Substrate-Conditional Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11153)
*Ramchand Kumaresan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在约1.5亿参数的widened-1536基座上，将进化混合LoRA系统分解为三个因子（路由器重写、逐域留一法评估、包含死亡/继承/突变/重分配的生命周期），通过5-of-8部分2^3析因实验（n=3种子，每细胞25000步）进行归因分析。结果表明：路由器重写独自贡献了完整进化系统对比静态B3基线全部+0.0426 nat的平衡对数困惑度改进（t=12.86, p=0.006），而生命周期是净拖累（约-0.028 nats, t=-4.46, p=0.047），逐域评估在种子分辨率上无效。辅助实验表明继承机制在修正聚合方式后统计不显著，基扰动实验方向性反驳了生命周期的"基因组上下文"解释框架。可控合成沙箱定位了底物条件性边界：进化搜索路由通道仅在适配器预对齐任务时起关键作用，其他情况下低于、持平或主动退化梯度解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对进化混合LoRA系统各组件进行严格归因分析，厘清各个因子（路由器重写、生命周期、评估范围）对整体性能改进的相对贡献，并检验"基因组上下文"等解释性假说。

Method: 采用5-of-8部分2^3析因实验设计（路由器重写×逐域评估×生命周期），在n=3种子、每细胞25000步条件下运行；辅以alpha=0继承反事实实验、基扰动探针实验和可控合成沙箱进行边界条件探测。

Result: 路由器重写单独贡献了全部改进（+0.0426 nats, t=12.86, p=0.006）；生命周期是净拖累（-0.028 nats, t=-4.46, p=0.047）；逐域评估无效；继承机制修正后统计不显著；进化搜索仅在适配器预对齐任务时有效。

Conclusion: 进化混合LoRA系统的性能改进完全来自路由器重写，生命周期机制实际有害，进化搜索路由的效用具有强烈的底物条件依赖性。

Abstract: We decompose an evolutionary mixture-of-LoRA system on a from-scratch ~150M-parameter widened-D substrate (D=1536, V=32000; D/V approx 0.048; the "widened-1536" substrate) into three factors -- a router rewrite (parallel sigmoid gate with learnable per-adapter floor and bounded temperature anneal, fed post-stack hidden states rather than token-embedding means), a per-domain leave-one-out evaluation scope, and a lifecycle of death plus alpha-blend inheritance plus SVD mutation plus slot reallocation -- and report a 5-of-8 partial 2^3 factorial run at n=3 seeds and 25000 adaptation steps per cell. The attribution chain is sharp on this substrate: the router rewrite carries the entire +0.0426 nat balanced log-PPL improvement (Delta = log PPL_ref - log PPL_test, positive = improvement; t=12.86, p=0.006) attributed to "the full evolutionary system vs the static B3 baseline"; the headline full-system-vs-B3 balanced contrast itself is +0.015 nats, t=1.94, p=0.19 at n=3 and does not clear alpha=0.05. The per-domain evaluation scope is null at seed-resolution, and the lifecycle is a net drag of approx -0.028 nats (t=-4.46,p=0.047 in the primary chain). An auxiliary alpha=0 inheritance counterfactual at n=3 seeds is sign-inconsistent at the headline metric and underpowered for either an equivalence or load-bearing conclusion (corrected from an earlier arithmetic-mean aggregator that erroneously cleared inheritance; see Appendix B.11). A base-perturbation probe directionally refutes a "genomic-context" reframe of the lifecycle role. A controllable synthetic sandbox locates a substrate-conditional regime boundary: evolutionary search on the routing channel is load-bearing only when adapters are pre-aligned to the task; in every other regime tested it underperforms, ties, or actively degrades the gradient solution.

</details>


### [136] [How Does Differential Privacy Affect Social Bias in LLMs? A Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11195)
*Eduardo Tenorio,Karuna Bhaila,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了差分隐私（DP）对大型语言模型（LLM）中社会偏见的影响，发现DP在句子评分任务中减少了偏见，但这一改善无法推广到所有任务；降低记忆化并不必然减少不公平性，强调了多范式评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）已被广泛用于保护LLM训练数据隐私，但其与模型社会偏见之间的关系尚不明确，现有研究对此缺乏系统理解。

Method: 使用DP-SGD训练预训练LLM，通过四个互补范式（句子评分、文本补全、表格分类、问答）系统比较DP模型与非DP基线在社会偏见上的表现。

Result: DP在句子评分任务中减少了偏见（通过控制似然比较测量），但该改善未泛化到所有任务；logit层面的偏见与输出层面的偏见存在不一致；降低记忆化并不必然减少不公平性。

Conclusion: DP对偏见的影响因任务而异，单一范式的评估不足以全面衡量公平性，评估LLM公平性时需采用多范式方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained on web-scale corpora can memorize sensitive training data, posing significant privacy risks. Differential privacy (DP) has emerged as a principled framework that limits the influence of individual data points during training, yet the relationship between differential privacy and social bias in LLMs remains poorly understood. To investigate this, we present a systematic evaluation of social bias in a pretrained LLM trained with DP-SGD, comparing a DP model against non-DP baselines across four complementary paradigms: sentence scoring, text completion, tabular classification, and question answering. We find that DP reduces bias in sentence scoring tasks, where bias is measured through controlled likelihood comparisons, yet this improvement does not generalize across all tasks. Our results reveal a discrepancy between logit-level bias and output-level bias. Moreover, decreasing memorization does not necessarily reduce unfairness, underscoring the importance of multi-paradigm evaluation when assessing fairness in LLMs.

</details>


### [137] [Instructions shape Production of Language, not Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11206)
*Andreas Waldis,Leshem Choshen,Yufang Hou,Yotam Perlit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言模型中指令触发的一种以产出为中心的机制：指令主要通过影响输出token（生产阶段）而非输入token（处理阶段）来驱动模型行为。通过五类二元判断任务的逐层探测，发现样本token中的任务相关信息稳定且与行为弱相关，而输出token中的信息变化大且与行为强相关。注意力干预实验验证了这种因果不对称性，且该模式随模型规模和指令微调而加剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从整体行为层面评估语言模型能力，但对指令如何内部驱动模型行为的具体机制尚不清楚。作者从认知科学中语言处理与生产的两阶段视角出发，探究指令token在输入处理与输出生产两个阶段中的信息塑造差异。

Method: 采用逐层探测（probing）方法，在五个二元判断任务上测量任务相关信息在样本token（输入处理阶段）和输出token（输出生产阶段）中的分布；通过注意力阻断干预进行因果验证；在多种模型家族、规模及指令微调变体上检验泛化性。

Result: 样本token中的任务相关信息稳定且与行为仅弱相关；输出token中的信息变化显著且与行为强相关；阻断指令流到所有后续token会同时降低行为表现和输出token信息，而仅阻断到样本token影响甚微；该不对称性跨模型、跨任务泛化，且随模型规模扩大和指令微调而增强。

Conclusion: 理解语言模型能力需要联合评估内部表征与外部行为，同时在内部视角中按token位置分解，区分输入token的处理过程与输出token的生产过程。

Abstract: Instructions trigger a production-centered mechanism in language models. Through a cognitively inspired lens that separates language processing and production, we reveal this mechanism as an asymmetry between the two stages by probing task-specific information layer-wise across five binary judgment tasks. Specifically, we measure how instruction tokens shape information both when sample tokens, the input under evaluation, are processed and when output tokens are produced. Across prompting variations, task-specific information in sample tokens remains largely stable and correlates only weakly with behavior, whereas the same information in output tokens varies substantially and correlates strongly with behavior. Attention-based interventions confirm this pattern causally: blocking instruction flow to all subsequent tokens reduces both behavior and information in output tokens, whereas blocking it only to sample tokens has minimal effect on either. The asymmetry generalizes across model families and tasks, and becomes sharper with model scale and instruction-tuning, both of which disproportionately affect the production stage. Our findings suggest that understanding model capabilities requires jointly assessing internals and behavior, while decomposing the internal perspective by token position to distinguish the processing of input tokens from the production of output tokens.

</details>


### [138] [RETUYT-INCO at BEA 2026 Shared Task 2: Meta-prompting in Rubric-based Scoring for German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11242)
*Ignacio Sastre,Ignacio Remersaro,Facundo Díaz,Nicolás De Horta,Luis Chiruzzo,Aiala Rosá,Santiago Góngora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RETUYT-INCO团队在BEA 2026共享任务“基于评分标准的德语短答案评分”中的参与情况。团队参与了三个赛道，提出了一种名为元提示（Meta-prompting）的方法，即让LLM根据训练集示例生成自定义提示来评分新答案。还尝试了经典机器学习、微调开源LLM和不同提示技术。最终在赛道1、3、4中分别获得第6名（QWK 0.729）、第4名（QWK 0.674）和第4名（QWK 0.490）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德语短答案自动评分需要根据特定评分标准进行，但不同任务（未见答案、未见问题）的评分标准变化较大，需要能够适应这种变化性的评分方法。

Method: 提出元提示（Meta-prompting）方法：让LLM基于训练集示例自动生成自定义评分提示，再用该提示对新的学生答案进行评分。此外还尝试了经典机器学习、微调开源LLM及不同提示工程技术。

Result: 赛道1（未见答案三分类）：第6/8名，QWK=0.729；赛道3（未见答案二分类）：第4/9名，QWK=0.674；赛道4（未见问题二分类）：第4/8名，QWK=0.490。

Conclusion: 元提示方法在德语短答案评分任务中展现了可行性，但相比其他参与者仍有提升空间，特别是在未见问题场景下（QWK仅0.49）表现有待改进。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the RETUYT-INCO participation at the BEA 2026 shared task "Rubric-based Short Answer Scoring for German". Our team participated in track 1 (Unseen answers three-way), track 3 (Unseen answers two-way) and track 4 (Unseen questions two-way). Since these tracks required scoring short student answers using specific rubrics, we looked for ways to handle the changing nature of the task. We created a method called Meta-prompting. In this approach, an LLM creates a custom prompt based on examples from the Train set. This prompt is then used to grade new student answers. Along with this method, we also describe other approaches we used, such as classic machine learning, fine-tuning open-source LLMs, and different prompting techniques. According to the official results, our team placed 6th out of 8 participants in Track 1 with a QWK of 0.729. In Track 3, we secured 4th place out of 9 with a QWK of 0.674, and we also placed 4th out of 8 in Track 4 with a QWK of 0.49.

</details>


### [139] [ReAD: Reinforcement-Guided Capability Distillation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11290)
*Xueqi Cheng,Xugui Zhou,Tyler Derr,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReAD框架，通过强化学习引导的能力蒸馏方法，在固定token预算下显式建模能力间的相互依赖关系，以提升下游任务效用并减少有害溢出和浪费的蒸馏开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能力蒸馏方法将能力视为独立训练目标，忽略了不同能力之间的相互影响，导致在固定预算下无法有效分配资源，且可能损害其他有用能力。

Method: ReAD框架包含三个步骤：首先推断任务关键能力；然后动态生成能力导向的监督信号；最后使用不确定性感知的上下文强盗算法，基于预期效用增益自适应分配蒸馏预算。

Result: 实验表明，在相同token预算下，ReAD相比强基线方法提升了下游任务效用，同时减少了有害溢出和浪费的蒸馏开销。

Conclusion: 显式建模能力间的相互依赖关系并自适应分配蒸馏预算，能够在能力蒸馏中更高效地利用有限资源，实现更好的下游任务表现。

Abstract: Capability distillation applies knowledge distillation to selected model capabilities, aiming to compress a large language model (LLM) into a smaller one while preserving the abilities needed for a downstream task. However, most existing methods treat capabilities as independent training targets and overlook how improving one capability can reshape the student's broader capability profile, especially when multiple abilities jointly determine task success. We study capability distillation under a fixed token budget and identify two consistent patterns: distillation induces systematic, budget-dependent cross-capability transfer, and additional budget often brings limited task-relevant gains while sometimes degrading other useful abilities. Building on these insights, we propose ReAD, a Reinforcement-guided cApability Distillation framework that explicitly accounts for capability interdependence. ReAD first infers task-essential capabilities, then generates capability-targeted supervision on the fly, and finally uses an uncertainty-aware contextual bandit to adaptively allocate the distillation budget based on expected utility gains. Extensive experiments show that ReAD improves downstream utility under the same token budget while reducing harmful spillover and wasted distillation effort compared to strong baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ReAD.

</details>


### [140] [Predicting Psychological Well-Being from Spontaneous Speech using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11303)
*Erfan Loweimi,Sofia de la Fuente Garcia,Saturnino Luz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用大语言模型（LLMs）从自发语音中零样本预测Ryff心理健康（PWB）分数。基于PsyVoiD数据库中111名参与者的几分钟语音记录，评估了12种指令微调LLM，包括Llama-3、Ministral、Mistral、Gemma-2/3、Phi-4、DeepSeek和QwQ-Preview等。采用与临床心理学和语言学专家合作开发的领域引导提示，结果显示LLM能从自发语音中提取语义线索，在80%数据上达到最高0.8的Spearman相关。同时通过统计分析揭示预测变异性和系统偏差，并利用关键词词云分析突出驱动模型预测的语言特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM在零样本条件下从自发语音中预测心理健康的可行性，弥补传统自评量表在生态效度和实时性方面的不足，推动自动化心理健康评估工具的发展。

Method: 使用PsyVoiD数据库中111名参与者的几分钟语音记录，评估12种指令微调LLM；与临床心理学和语言学专家合作设计领域引导提示；采用Spearman相关评估预测性能；通过统计分析和词云分析进行可解释性研究。

Result: LLM能有效从自发语音中提取语义线索进行心理健康预测，在80%数据上Spearman相关系数最高达0.8；不同模型间存在预测变异性和系统偏差。

Conclusion: LLM在零样本自发语音心理健康评估中展现出潜力，可作为一种有效的自动化评估工具，但需进一步研究模型偏差和预测变异性以提升可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: We investigate the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) for zero-shot prediction of Ryff Psychological Well-Being (PWB) scores from spontaneous speech. Using a few minutes of voice recordings from 111 participants in the PsyVoiD database, we evaluated 12 instruction-tuned LLMs, including Llama-3 (8B, 70B), Ministral, Mistral, Gemma-2-9B, Gemma-3 (1B, 4B, 27B), Phi-4, DeepSeek (Qwen and Llama), and QwQ-Preview. A domain-informed prompt was developed in collaboration with experts in clinical psychology and linguistics. Results show that LLMs can extract semantically meaningful cues from spontaneous speech, achieving Spearman correlations of up to 0.8 on 80\% of the data. Additionally, to enhance explainability, we conducted statistical analyses to characterise prediction variability and systematic biases, alongside keyword-based word cloud analyses to highlight the linguistic features driving the models' predictions.

</details>


### [141] [SOMA: Efficient Multi-turn LLM Serving via Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11317)
*Xueqi Cheng,Qiong Wu,Zhengyi Zhou,Xugui Zhou,Tyler Derr,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SOMA框架，利用对话前期回合估计局部响应流形，通过软提示学习、反退化训练和局部LoRA微调，将大模型能力迁移到小模型上，实现多轮对话中质量与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多轮对话中拼接完整历史导致高延迟、高内存和高API成本，现有方法难以在响应质量与效率之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 1) 利用对话早期回合估计局部响应流形；2) 学习软提示最大化大模型与小模型之间的语义分歧，发现最不对齐的局部方向；3) 反退化控制稳定训练；4) 将挖掘的案例蒸馏到局部LoRA微调中，推理时无需提示；5) 简单门控实现一次性切换并支持回滚。

Result: 大量实验验证了SOMA的有效性。

Conclusion: SOMA通过局部适配小模型，在不牺牲对话质量的前提下显著提升多轮对话服务的效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-turn dialogue settings where preserving conversational context across turns is essential. A standard serving practice concatenates the full dialogue history at every turn, which reliably maintains coherence but incurs substantial cost in latency, memory, and API expenditure, especially when queries are routed to large proprietary models. Existing approaches often struggle to balance the trade-off between response quality and efficiency. We propose a framework that exploits the early turns of a session to estimate a local response manifold and then adapt a smaller surrogate model to this local region for the remainder of the conversation. Concretely, we learn soft prompts that maximize semantic divergence between the large and surrogate small language models' responses to surface least-aligned local directions, stabilize training with anti-degeneration control, and distill the mined cases into localized LoRA fine-tuning so the surrogate runs without prompts at inference. A simple gate enables a one-time switch with rollback on drift. We further provide a theoretical analysis for key components in SOMA. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of SOMA. The source code is provided at: https://github.com/LabRAI/SOMA.

</details>


### [142] [Large Language Models for Causal Relations Extraction in Social Media: A Validation Framework for Disaster Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11348)
*Ujun Jeong,Saketh Vishnubhatla,Bohan Jiang,Andre Harrison,Adrienne Raglin,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）从灾难相关的社交媒体帖子中提取因果关系的有效性，提出了基于专家评估的框架，并分析了提取的关系是否基于事后的证据或模型先验。结果揭示了LLMs在灾难决策支持系统中应用的潜力与风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在灾难期间，从社交媒体中提取因果关系有助于增强态势感知，识别与人员伤亡、物理损害、基础设施中断及级联影响相关的因素。然而，灾难相关的帖子通常是非正式的、碎片化的且依赖上下文，可能描述个人经历而非明确的因果关系。现有方法难以有效处理此类非结构化的因果信息。

Method: (1) 提出了一个基于专家评估的框架，将LLMs生成的因果图与来自灾难特定报告的参考图进行比较；(2) 评估了提取的因果关系是来自事后的证据还是反映了模型的先验知识。

Result: 研究发现LLMs在灾难社交媒体因果提取中既有潜力也存在风险，提示了其在灾难决策支持系统中应用的复杂性与不确定性。

Conclusion: LLMs可用于灾难社交媒体的因果提取，但需谨慎对待其输出，确保提取的关系有事后证据支撑，而非仅依赖模型先验，否则可能引入误导性信息。

Abstract: During disasters, extracting causal relations from social media can strengthen situational awareness by identifying factors linked to casualties, physical damage, infrastructure disruption, and cascading impacts. However, disaster-related posts are often informal, fragmented, and context-dependent, and they may describe personal experiences rather than explicit causal relations. In this work, we examine whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can effectively extract causal relations from disaster-related social media posts. To this end, we (1) propose an expert-grounded evaluation framework that compares LLM-generated causal graphs with reference graphs derived from disaster-specific reports and (2) assess whether the extracted relations are supported by post-event evidence or instead reflect model priors. Our findings highlight both the potential and risks of using LLMs for causal relation extraction in disaster decision-support systems.

</details>


### [143] [An Empirical Study of Automating Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11378)
*Kang Zhou,Sangmin Woo,Haibo Ding,Kiran Ramnath,Subramanian Chidambaram,Aosong Feng,Vinayak Arannil,Muhyun Kim,Ishan Singh,Darren Wang,Zhichao Xu,Megha Gandhi,Nirmal Prabhu,Soumya Smruti Mishra,Vivek Singh,Gouri Pandeshwar,Lin Lee Cheong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalAgent，一个自动化智能体评估流程的AI助手，通过编码评估领域知识为可复用的评估技能，将首次运行即成功执行且有意义的评估率从17.5%提升至65%，并获得79.5%的人类专家偏好率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评估涉及多步复杂行为（工具使用、中间推理），成本高且需要专业知识。前沿编码助手虽编码能力强，但缺乏领域特定评估知识，无法可靠地自动化评估流程：执行成功率仅30%，且生成过度工程化的评估（平均12+指标）。

Method: 提出EvalAgent，将评估领域专业知识编码为可组合的评估技能（程序化指令、可复用代码和模板、动态检索的API文档），构建基于轨迹的管道，生成完整的评估工件（指标、可执行代码、报告）。同时提出元评估框架AgentEvalBench（20个智能体，每个配评估需求和测试场景）和Eval@1指标（衡量生成的评估代码首次运行是否执行成功并产生有意义结果）。

Result: EvalAgent将Eval@1从17.5%提升至65%，达到79.5%的人类专家偏好率。消融实验表明，移除评估技能后Eval@1从65%骤降至30%，验证了评估技能对处理复杂评估的关键作用。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖编码能力无法可靠地自动化智能体评估，需要将领域评估知识系统化编码为可复用技能。EvalAgent通过这种方法显著提升了自动化评估的质量和可靠性。

Abstract: Agent evaluation requires assessing complex multi-step behaviors involving tool use and intermediate reasoning, making it costly and expertise-intensive. A natural question arises: can frontier coding assistants reliably automate this evaluation process? Our study shows that simply prompting coding assistants is insufficient for this task. Without domain-specific evaluation knowledge, frontier coding assistants achieve only a 30% execution success rate and produce over-engineered evaluations averaging 12+ metrics per agent, indicating that strong coding ability does not automatically translate to reliable agent evaluation. We introduce EvalAgent, an AI assistant that automates the end-to-end agent evaluation pipeline.
